A repeat returns matching existing assignments without notifying again. No browser or app deployment.',\n+  inputSchema: guidedSchema,\n+  annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true },\n+}, plan => guidedCommand(plan, false));\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/system/scripts/lsatu-mcp-call.mjs\n+// Immediate local MCP client while a chat still has the older tool snapshot.\n+import fs from 'node:fs';\n+import path from 'node:path';\n+import { createRequire } from 'node:module';\n+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\n+import { projectPath, vaultRoot } from './project-paths.mjs';\n+const req = createRequire(projectPath('lsatu', 'package.json'));\n+const [{ Client }, { StdioClientTransport }] = await Promise.all([\n+  import(pathToFileURL(req.resolve('@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js')).href),\n+  import(pathToFileURL(req.resolve('@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js')).href),\n+]);\n+const [name, flag, value] = process.argv.slice(2);\n+const allowed = ['search_guided_drills', 'preview_guided_drill_batch', 'assign_guided_drill_batch'];\n+if (!allowed.includes(name) || !['--file', '--json'].includes(flag) || !value) throw new Error('Usage: node lsatu-mcp-call.mjs <guided tool> --file <arguments.json> | --json <single-line JSON>');\n+const args = JSON.parse(flag === '--file' ? fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(value), 'utf8') : value);\n+const transport = new StdioClientTransport({ command: process.execPath, args: [path.join(vaultRoot, 'system/scripts/lsatu-lesson-mcp.mjs')], cwd: vaultRoot, stderr: 'pipe' });\n+const client = new Client({ name: 'ai-os-guided-operator', version: '1.0.0' });\n+try {\n+  await client.connect(transport);\n+  const result = await client.callTool({ name, arguments: args }, undefined, { timeout: 180000 });\n+  if (result.isError) process.exitCode = 1;\n+  console.log(JSON.stringify(result.structuredContent ?? result));\n+} finally {\n+  await client.close();\n+}\n*** End Patch"));

It covers accounts and upkeep: new signups (\\"who signed up\\", \\"did anyone new join\\"), access (\\"give Venusa access\\", \\"turn on drills for X\\", \\"what can X see\\"), archiving, moving a due date, unassigning, booking passes (\\"how many sessions does Sandra have left\\"), notifications, and reading or marking messages (\\"who messaged me\\", \\"show my thread with Celia\\"). It is the one place for any operation on the LSAT U platform, and none of it uses Lovable.\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# assign-lsatu-work (pointer)\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is a pointer. The full skill lives in ONE place:\\r\\n\\r\\n    C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\.agents\\\\skills\\\\assign-lsatu-work\\\\SKILL.md\\r\\n    (identical to C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\.claude\\\\skills\\\\assign-lsatu-work\\\\SKILL.md, which Claude uses)\\r\\n\\r\\n**Read that file in full before every LSAT U operation, and follow it exactly.**\\r\\nDo not copy its contents here; this file must stay a pointer so the two runtimes never drift.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe short version, in case the file cannot be read:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Every LSAT U operation runs headless through ONE command, from the vault root, with bash:\\r\\n  `bash system/scripts/lsatu <command> ...`\\r\\n  Examples: `bash system/scripts/lsatu status \\"Celia\\"`,\\r\\n  `bash system/scripts/lsatu assign --student \\"Celia\\" --section PT156.S2 --due 2026-09-30`.\\r\\n- No browser, no Chrome, no Lovable for routine content or assignments. Use\\r\\n  `lsat-u-platform-actions` for a complete interactive Classroom lesson: its\\r\\n  local MCP posts the HTML directly and verifies the saved copy headlessly.\\r\\n  This skill handles other assignments and student operations.\\r\\n- Reads never need permission. One named student assign / message: run it, then report.\\r\\n  Confirm first: 3+ students, revoke/archive/unarchive/unassign/all-off, unsend.\\r\\n- Never pass a multi-line argument. On Windows a newline drops `--dry-run` and sends for real\\r\\n  (this happened on 2026-09-23). Never hand-write assignment rows.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"819b46","wall_time_seconds":0.0000108,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":663,"output":"# Skill mechanics\\r\\n\\r\\nThe skill-specific branch of [`writing-for-agents`](SKILL.md): what changes when the document is a skill (frontmatter, the invocation choice, and router skills). Everything else about writing it is the universal reference in `SKILL.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Invocation\\r\\n\\r\\nTwo choices, trading the two loads:\\r\\n\\r\\n- A **model-invoked** skill keeps a `description`, so the agent can fire it autonomously, and other skills can reach it. You can still type its name: model-invocation always _includes_ user reach; a description only ever adds agent discovery, never removes the human\'s. The description is the skill\'s top-level context pointer, forced to stay loaded at all times: permanent context load in exchange for discoverability. A model-invoked skill whose content is all reference is also one home for shared reference: another skill can invoke it, so reference needed by several skills lives in one place. Mechanics: omit `disable-model-invocation`, and write a model-facing description carrying the trigger branches (the pointer-writing rules in `SKILL.md` apply in full).\\r\\n- A **user-invoked** skill strips the description from the agent\'s reach: only the human typing its name can invoke it, and no other skill can. Zero context load, but it spends cognitive load: you are the index that must remember it exists. Mechanics: set `disable-model-invocation: true`; the `description` becomes human-facing: a one-line summary, trigger lists stripped.\\r\\n\\r\\nPick model-invocation only when the agent must reach the skill on its own, or another skill must. If it only ever fires by hand, make it user-invoked and pay no context load.\\r\\n\\r\\nShared reference that two user-invoked skills both need can live in neither: with no descriptions, neither can fire the other. Push it to a plain file outside the skill system: external reference any skill can point at.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Splitting by invocation\\r\\n\\r\\nThe invocation cut of splitting (the sequence cut lives in `SKILL.md`): split off a model-invoked skill when you have a distinct leading word that should trigger it on its own (a trigger word you actually use in your prompts), or another skill must reach it. You pay context load for the new always-loaded description, so that independent reach has to be worth it.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Router skills\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen user-invoked skills multiply past what you can remember, that piled-up cognitive load is cured by a **router skill**: one user-invoked skill that names the others and when to reach for each, so the human has one skill to remember instead of many. It can only hint, never fire them: user-invoked skills have no description, so nothing but the human can reach them.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"2ccb84","wall_time_seconds":10.0047462,"session_id":12772,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

## Tool: exec

const body=`# Assign guided drills through the local MCP

Use this branch for guided-drill selection, difficulty matching, or a multi-day schedule. Other LSAT U operations keep their existing global skill route.

## Before executing

1. Reuse the student's verified identity, dates and teaching goal from this chat. One explicitly named student is authorized; reads and preview do not need approval. Retrieve student context only when the teaching goal is unclear. Check live existing assignments through batch preview rather than repeatedly reading every ledger or transcript.
2. Search \`search_guided_drills\` on the existing local \`lsat-u-lessons\` MCP. Send a short query and return a small candidate list. The generated [catalog](../../../../references/lsatu-guided-drill-catalog.md) maps every drill; its JSON holds complete cards and filters. Refresh with \`node system/scripts/refresh-guided-drill-catalog.mjs\` when the snapshot is old, content changed, or no suitable candidate appears. Assignment preflight always reads live records and the deployed merged question bank.
3. Prefer existing drills. Match the actual eligible pool and difficulty ceiling, not only “Easier,” “Medium,” or a catalog level. Beginner is 1–3; intermediate is 2–4; advanced is 3–5. With sufficient in-band questions the app selects only that band. Example: a native 3–4 drill at beginner becomes difficulty 3 only. Preview refuses insufficient in-band pools and any selectable question above \`max_difficulty\`.
4. Build one structured plan containing the exact student and all dates/slugs. Keep the native question count unless Joshua specifies a shorter drill. A count override does not rewrite the existing instruction card. Exact catalog pins override level/count, so preview verifies their actual content. Pool drills redraw on reattempt and can overlap other drills; do not promise unique questions across days.
5. Run \`preview_guided_drill_batch\` once. Completion criterion: every item resolves to the intended student, published content, enough eligible questions, correct effective difficulty/count, valid unlock/due dates, and either a new assignment or a matching existing one. Preflight all entries before any write. Use one writer at a time against the operational checkout.

## Execute and verify

Call \`assign_guided_drill_batch\` once with the same plan. It syncs the pinned operational checkout once, signs in once, resolves the student once, reads the catalog and deployed bank once, and calls the app's own \`assignWork()\` serially. It verifies source rows, Classroom visibility, local unlock/due dates, and bell count. Each new assignment retains normal inbox, bell and email notification behavior.

A repeat returns matching existing rows without notifying again. Conflicting existing content, unexpected assignment pins, duplicates, or ambiguous identity fail explicitly. Partial failures preserve completed assignment IDs and notification results. Inspect uncertain state before retrying. A failed notification channel does not justify reassignment. Email OK is platform send acceptance, not independently verified receipt. Headless verification is not a browser runner launch.

New tools may need a fresh chat/MCP connection before appearing in the model's tool list. Immediate fallback uses the same local MCP, without a browser:

\`\`\`powershell
node system/scripts/lsatu-mcp-call.mjs preview_guided_drill_batch --file students/<student>/assignments/<plan>.json
node system/scripts/lsatu-mcp-call.mjs assign_guided_drill_batch --file students/<student>/assignments/<plan>.json
\`\`\`

Plan shape:

\`\`\`json
{
  "id": "student-conditionals-YYYY-MM-DD",
  "student": "Exact platform name",
  "assignments": [
    {
      "drill": "exact-existing-slug",
      "schedule": "YYYY-MM-DD",
      "due": "YYYY-MM-DD",
      "level": "beginner",
      "max_difficulty": 3
    }
  ]
}
\`\`\`

Store plans and result evidence under the student's \`assignments/\` folder. Log assignments and notification sends once; update the session handoff. Keep Friday/day-off dates and session-day exclusions explicit.

## After executing

Report assigned work, effective difficulty/count, dates and material notification limitations. Use returned metrics to review the pathway: repeated setup, broad retrieval, custom content that an existing drill could cover, or duplicate verification. Fix deterministic mistakes in the tool; add only a short new instruction for a judgment rule. Prefer one shared batch operation and a catalog pointer over more per-day commands or copied drill metadata.

## Authored content exception

Only when existing drills cannot meet the requested content, use the existing \`--guided-spec <absolute JSON path>\` alongside \`--drill <matching slug>\`. This authored-spec path currently validates conditional Must Be True questions; it is not the general conditionals catalog path. Use the app's assignment function, then \`verify-guided-spec.mjs\`. Do not hand-write assignment rows or claim browser QA. No Lovable/app deployment is needed for these local content operations.
`;
for(const p of [".agents/skills/assign-lsatu-work/references/guided-drill-workflow.md","skills/codex/assign-lsatu-work/references/guided-drill-workflow.md","skills/claude/assign-lsatu-work/references/guided-drill-workflow.md"]){
text(await tools.apply_patch(`*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/${p}\n+${body.trimEnd().split("\n").join("\n+")}\n*** End Patch`));
}
const tailold="## AI OS authored guided sets (2026-09-30)";
for(const p of [".agents/skills/assign-lsatu-work/SKILL.md","skills/codex/assign-lsatu-work/SKILL.md","skills/claude/assign-lsatu-work/SKILL.md"]){
text(await tools.apply_patch(`*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/${p}\n@@\n-# assign-lsatu-work (pointer)\n+# Assign LSAT U work\n+\n+For guided-drill selection or schedules, follow [the guided-drill workflow](references/guided-drill-workflow.md). Use the mapped catalog, one live preview and one batch MCP assignment. This local branch contains its authorization and verification rules; do not load the global reference for a routine guided schedule.\n@@\n-**Read that file in full before every LSAT U operation, and follow it exactly.**\n+For other LSAT U operations, read that file in full and follow it exactly.\n@@\n-## AI OS authored guided sets (2026-09-30)\n-\n-For exact teacher-selected guided questions, add \`--guided-spec <absolute JSON path>\` alongside \`--drill <matching slug>\`. Store the authored specification under the student's \`assignments/\` folder. The wrapper validates complete conditional Must Be True questions and their declared difficulty bands, creates or reuses the matching catalog content, and still calls the app's own \`assignWork()\` for assignments and notifications. It refuses a duplicate assignment for the same student, drill, and date. Run a dry run first.\n-\n-Verify with \`node system/scripts/verify-guided-spec.mjs <exact student name> <spec.json> ...\`. It checks saved content, exact deployed question types and difficulty, student access, one assignment, Classroom visibility, local dates, and one bell notification. It also accepts \`PT101.S3@2026-10-04\` to verify a timed section. CLI notification output remains the email evidence. This is headless verification; do not claim a browser launch check. Never hand-write assignment rows.\n+Guided-drill catalog and MCP details live in the [guided workflow reference](references/guided-drill-workflow.md), including the authored-content exception and immediate fallback when new MCP tools have not appeared in a chat yet.\n*** End Patch`));
}

Snapshot metadata includes catalog/bank hashes and normalization-source commit.\n+- Partial failures preserve assignment IDs and channel outcomes. Reuse checks reject unexpected assignment pins and conflicting unlock/due dates.\n+- The [project skill](../.agents/skills/assign-lsatu-work/SKILL.md) now routes routine guided schedules to a short local recipe, bypassing the long global reference. Codex activation and both runtime catalog copies match.\n+\n+Use one writer per operational checkout. The MCP serializes calls within its process; cross-process concurrent writers are not supported. New tool names need a fresh MCP connection to appear in a chat's tool list; lsatu-mcp-call.mjs invokes the same server immediately. Independent email receipt and student-session runner launch remain unverified. Tasks status, app code and deployment are outside this operation.\n*** End Patch"));
